    - **Meta.** `case.updated-tags` carries the public `context.added-tags` and `context.removed-tags`. `case.updated-fields` carries `context.field-keys`. `case.status-updated` carries `context.from` and `to`, as status keys. `case.exported` carries `context.case-export-id`. `case-template.deleted-field` carries `field-key` and `version-number`.
    - **No value.** A case event's `fields` and a comment event's `message` are `null` (`carriesNoValue`), and case events carry no `included`, as run events carry none.
    - **Stubs, groups and filters.** `case` and `case-comment` have redacted stubs. The dashboard groups are "Cases" (with the template event) and "Case comments". The webhook contract gains `case-template-ids`, and `foundation-8b`'s resolver applies C143.4's every-held-inquiry rule.
25. **Templates (C130).**
    - **Field schemas.** A version's `field-schemas` refuse `file` and `json` at any depth the walker allows (`caseFieldTypeProblems`), and `caseFieldSchemaProblems` adds the walker's own rules for publish.
    - **Statuses.** The schema holds a version's statuses to distinct keys, exactly one `initial` and at least one `end`, and holds `transitions` to declared keys. Publish adds what needs a walk: an `end` status unreachable from `initial`.
    - **The template.** It serves `published-version` and `draft-version` as numbers, with relationships to the version ids. Its `key` never changes. `layout-modules` are `{ key, required-permissions }` over a closed list of thirteen modules. The attachment policy is at most 20 files of the four types.
26. **Case actions (C140.1).**
    - **The binding.** An action binds a workflow to 1 to 50 templates. `header` is the only placement. A prefill names a `case.fields.`, `inquiry.fields.` or `account.fields.` path, never a value.
    - **The routes.** `run-action` and `available-actions` mirror the account paths, `form` included, because `parameters` is PII.
27. **The case step contracts (C140.3).** `packages/workflow-engine/src/actions/cases.ts`:
    - **The exports.** `CASE_ACTION_CONTRACTS`, `createCaseActionEntries()`, `CASE_ACTION_OBJECT_TYPE_ENTRY` and `CASE_ACTION_SINK_ROWS`.
    - **The entries.** Each is internal, exactly once, never adverse and never retried. Each grant is `cases:write`, and `cases:assign` for `assign_case`. `assign_case` names a user or a policy, one of them.
    - **What is plain.** A status, a resolution, a status tag and a tag are plain sinks, and a comment and a field take PII. There is no `create_case.name` (amendment 8).
    - **Time.** The SLA steps take `{ value, unit }`, with `unit` one of `minutes`, `hours` or `days`. The service holds the 90 days.
    - **The join.** `foundation-8b` joins the four in `apps/api/src/workflows/definitions/registry.ts`, with a handler stub for each key in the same commit. `WORKFLOW_ACTION_ERROR_TYPES` gains `not_implemented` (`configuration-error`), so a stub's error type does not lie, and the case codes a step can meet, each typed by the table's rule. `docs/workflows/errors.md` is regenerated.
28. **The graph's `create-case` (C140.6).** It joins `GRAPH_BULK_ACTIONS` and `graphQueryActionAttributesSchema` with `case-template-id` (and an optional `case-queue-id`). The answer gains an optional, nullable `case-id`. `authorize` asks `cases:write`. The action answers 501 until `api-case-workflows` replaces that one `case` of `services/graph/actions.ts`, so a preview counts and nothing is made. The console's Records and switches hold the action. `abilities` is unchanged, so the console does not offer it until 8C's button.
29. **PII lists for `foundation-8b`.**
    - **The lists.** `PII_CASE_ATTRIBUTES` (`fields`, `creator-email-address`, `statement`) and `PII_CASE_COMMENT_ATTRIBUTES` (`message`). Each joins `MASKED_ATTRIBUTES`, the API-log set and `MASKED_ATTRIBUTE_NAMES`, where amendment 4 applies.
    - **At any depth.** `message`, `statement` and `query` join the stored log's `MASKED_MEMBERS`, because they sit in `meta` or at a body's top, where an attribute list does not reach. That also nulls a run error's fixed `message` in a stored log, which fails closed.
    - **Already masked.** `comment` is masked already. `query` is `PII_REPORT_ATTRIBUTES`'.
    - **In call 2.** `user-email-address` and the attachment names arrive with (Q) and (F).
30. **Existing tests changed with the plan,** none weakened:
    - `ids.test.ts` in both packages, `permissions.test.ts`, and `vocabulary.test.ts` in core and contracts;
    - the dashboard's `permissions.test.ts`, `role-options.test.ts` and `events.test.ts`, and `role.json`;
    - `rate-limits.test.ts` and `plugins/rate-limit.test.ts`;
    - `workflow-runs.test.ts` (the creator), `graph-queries.test.ts` in core and contracts (`create-case`) and `fixtures.test.ts`;
    - `events.test.ts`, `pii-collisions.test.ts`, `workflows.test.ts` (the filter) and `resources.test.ts` (the node groups);
    - `ContractFixturesTest.kt` (28 serverOnly entries), and `process-government-id.test.ts` (a comment joined the payload union).
31. **Two load-sensitive tests, for their owners (rule 6).** Both failed once inside a forced non-API run at a load of up to 13.5, and passed alone, under 48 busy loops, and in the end-of-call gate, so neither was reproduced:
    - `packages/workflow-engine/src/code-runner/socket.test.ts`, "answers on the same connection". Its `dial()` and the test wait with `expect.poll`'s 1 s default, while the code it tests allows a child 20 s to start (`CHILD_START_TIMEOUT_MS`). The likely fix: wait for the frame by its `data` event, bounded by the test's own guard.
    - `apps/flow/src/steps/selfie-capture-step.test.tsx`, "takes each side pose once the head turned from the user's own centre frame". `waitFor(onSubmitted)` passed its 10 s. A probe showed the "hold still" before the right turn is not left over from the left pose, so the cause is still open.


## Workflow 8B Stage 1b amendments (foundation-8b)

Recorded by `foundation-8b`. The evidence is `docs/tdd/phase8-foundation-8b.tdd.md`. Each item binds every later 8B agent. Items marked (default) are on the owner's review list.

### Checkpoint (C) cases

1. **Tables beyond the (C) list.** `case_action_templates` holds the 1 to 50 templates of an action (C140.1), with composite keys to both, because an array column could name another environment's template. `case_bulk_actions` (`cblk_`, Stage 1a amendment 5) holds a bulk action: its request sealed (`case_bulk_actions.request#<id>`, because a search can name field values and a status change a comment), its counts, and its outcomes as case ids and codes, read by its creator alone. A document is a document verification (H31), held in `case_verifications` with `held_as` `document` rather than in a table of its own.
2. **`cases`.** `case_queue_id` has a format check and no foreign key until checkpoint (Q) adds `case_queues`; the SLA columns arrive with (Q) too. The assignee column is `assignee_id`, a `usr_` id (C132.3's index names `assignee_user_id`; (Q) indexes `assignee_id`). `cases_resolved_in_end`: `resolved_at` is set exactly while the status's category is `end` (C131.1). Every actor is a pair whole or not at all, its id's prefix following its type (`usr`, `key`, `wfr`, `cap`), and `name` reads `<KEY>-<n>`. Names are unique per environment, and a case carries `workflow_run_step_id` with its key (C117.5), as `case_comments` does.
3. **`case_history`.** Its row number (a `bigint` identity) is its sequence and its served id. Its actor types are the case's, plus `case-assignment-policy` and `system` (no id). `openverify_case_history_append_only` refuses every UPDATE, with no redaction escape, because a row holds no value (C131.4, C139.1), and a DELETE outside `openverify.environment_deletion` or `openverify.retention`. `CASE_HISTORY_KINDS` is a CHECK, so a kind can never be a value.
4. **Repository stubs and their owners** (`packages/db/src/repositories/`, exported from its index; each rejects with `CaseRepositoryNotBuiltError` naming its owner, `case-repository.ts`). `api-cases`: `cases.ts` (`createCaseRow`, `getCaseRow`, `lockCaseRow`, `nextCaseNumber`, `listCasesAfter`), `case-templates.ts`, `case-comments.ts`, `case-history.ts`, `case-objects.ts` (`listCasesHolding`, `attachCaseObjects`), `case-field-index.ts`, `notifications.ts`, `case-bulk-actions.ts`. `api-case-workflows`: `case-actions.ts` (not in its bullet's list; ours) and `case-references.ts`. A builder may widen a stub's signature while nothing calls it. Seeds by SQL: `seedCaseTemplate`, `seedCase`, `seedCaseComment`, `seedCaseBulkAction` in `@openverify/db/testing`.
5. **The webhook filters (C143.4).** `case-template-ids` compares a subject's `caseTemplateId`. A case's held inquiries come as `heldInquiryTemplateIds`, and then the every-held rule replaces the any-one rule for `inquiry-template-ids`: each template must be allowed (`?&`), and a strict case holding no inquiry fails a present filter. The same in core's `matchesWebhookFilters`, in `services/webhooks.ts`' stored filters (which dropped the key before) and in the trigger filters' keys (`workflows/triggers/deliver.ts`). `events/subject.ts` sends `case` and `case-comment` payloads to `resolveCaseFilterSubjects` (`services/cases/filter-subjects.ts`, `api-cases`) and makes them strict, so until it reads a case its events fail any present filter.
6. **Masking (C143.5, Stage 1a amendment 29).** `PII_CASE_ATTRIBUTES` and `PII_CASE_COMMENT_ATTRIBUTES` join `MASKED_ATTRIBUTES`, the stored log's attribute set and `MASKED_ATTRIBUTE_NAMES`. `message`, `statement` and `query` join `MASKED_MEMBERS`, so a report's `query` attribute is masked whole in a stored log too, which fails closed. `REDACTION_SUBJECT_ID_PREFIXES` gains `case` and `cscm`, and the three sealed columns join `ENCRYPTED_COLUMNS`, `SEALED` and `REWRAP_TABLES`.
7. **The catalogue (Stage 1a amendment 27).** `workflows/definitions/registry.ts` joins the case row, the eight entries and the case sinks (`WORKFLOW_SINKS`, passed as `context.sinks`). `CASE_ACTION_HANDLERS` (`workflows/actions/cases/index.ts`) joins `HANDLERS`, each a `transaction` stub that throws `ActionStepError('not_implemented')`. The entries' `availability` is `none`, so `GET /workflow-actions` lists the case actions as available and a published case step ends its run with `not_implemented` (`configuration-error`), never a success. (Default.) `docs/workflows/actions.md` is regenerated.
8. **The notifications are the dashboard API's.** `GET /api/v1/dashboard/notifications` and `POST /api/v1/dashboard/notifications/:id/read` are registered in `routes/dashboard/index.ts` behind the operator's session, with `cases:read`, because `/api/v1/dashboard/*` is the dashboard API (`openapi.test.ts` holds that path to its rules). Their services take `(db, UserPrincipal, …)`. An API key has no notifications.
9. **Where the 8A joins are** (orchestrator decision 5 after Workflow 8A), each a call to a stub that answers what the 8A file answered: `definitions/context.ts` (`currentCaseScopeOf`) and `engine/advance-run.ts`' `beginRun` (`currentCaseValueOf`, `workflows/actions/cases/current-case.ts`); `runs/test-runs.ts` needs none, because it reuses `loadCheckContext` and `beginRun`. `actions/objects/table.ts` (`CASE_OBJECT_ROWS`), `actions/decisions/case-holds.ts` (`caseHoldsSubject`), `triggers/objects.ts` (`caseTriggerDocumentOf`), `triggers/service.ts` (`caseObjectIdFits`; `objectIdFits` is exported), `publish/references.ts` (`CASE_KIND_OF_PREFIX`, `CASE_REFERENCE_MEMBERS`), `publish/rules.ts` (`caseTemplateContestRouteIssues`), `recipes/rows.ts` (`CASE_RECIPE_ROWS`), all in `workflows/actions/cases/` (`api-case-workflows`); `repositories/workflow-schedules.ts` (`listCasesAfter`, `api-cases`) and `repositories/workflow-versions.ts` (`CASE_WORKFLOW_REFERENCE_KINDS`, `caseReferenceSource`, `api-case-workflows`). `actions/objects/redact.ts` sends a `case` to `RedactionService.redactCase`. `case-joins.test.ts` in the db package and the API replaces each stub and checks the 8A file answers it.
10. **Case redaction (C139.1, F35).** `RedactionService` gains `redactCase(ctx, caseId, actor)`: `redactCasesInTx` (`services/cases/redaction.ts`, `api-cases`) in one transaction with the case's runs (C120.9, on a first redaction), 404 when the stub finds no case, objects deleted after the commit. The case's own audit entry is `redactCasesInTx`'s, because only it knows a first redaction. A transaction's `case` child goes through `redactCasesInTx` with actor `null` and `cause: { transaction-id }` (C127.7, outcome 28). `redactRunsForSubjectsInTx` hands the copies' targets to `redactCaseCopiesInTx` and leaves the ones it redacted out of `workflow-run.redaction-copies-found`.
11. **`filterCaseVisibility(db, { environmentId, principal }, items, caseIdsOf)`** (`services/cases/visibility.ts`, with `caseIdsIn`, which finds `case_` and `cscm_` ids in any value) is called by `GET /events` and `GET /events/:id`, `GET /api-logs` and `GET /api-logs/:id` (by `resource_id` and `subject_ids`), and `listWorkflowRuns` and `requireRun` (by the run's subjects), so every run route behind `requireRun` is covered. A page can come back short and `links.next` still moves on. `listUserNotifications` calls it inside `api-cases`' service. `GET /workflow-trigger-deliveries` is left for `api-cases` to decide (inventory 13.4).
12. **A key's `case-template-ids` (C129.4).** `createApiKey` and `cloneApiKey` call `assertKeyTemplateRestrictionInTx(tx, environmentId, creator, ids)` (`services/cases/key-restriction.ts`, `api-cases`) before the insert; a clone holds the source's list to its new creator. Until `api-cases` builds it, a non-empty list answers 501, because an unchecked list could see more than its creator. The resource serves the member only when the key holds a list.
13. **Hooks `api-cases` calls**, each declared in its owner's path: `createCaseInTx(tx, deps, input)` (`services/cases/create.ts`, `api-cases`, with `fakeCaseCreation()` in `services/cases/testing.ts`) is the one creation path of `POST /cases`, the review request, the graph bulk action and `create_case`, and the `case-create` limit is its caller's; `applySlaOnStatusChangeInTx(tx, change)` (`services/cases/sla/status-hook.ts`, `api-queues-sla`) on every status change; `assignOnEntry(tx, entry)` (`services/cases/assignment/on-entry.ts`, `api-queues-sla`), answering the assigned user or `null`; `acceptPatchAttachments(tx, target, attachments)` (`services/cases/attachments/patch.ts`, `api-case-files`), which answers 501 for any attachment until built, so none is dropped silently.
14. **The `case-bulk` queue (C137.4, C143.2).** `jobs/case-bulk-queue.ts` (`CASE_BULK_QUEUE`, `createCaseBulkQueue`; ids only, job id the `cblk_` id, removed on completion and failure), `ApiDependencies.caseBulk` (the container builds it), and the worker's `cases` option registering `processCaseBulkAction(job, deps)` (`services/cases/bulk/process.ts`, `api-cases`), a stub that finds nothing to do. The plan names no sweep for it; `api-cases` decides whether a pending row needs one.
15. **Retention (C139.3, C134.4).** `runCaseRetention({ db, storage, events }, { now, limit })` (`services/cases/retention.ts`, `api-cases`, the reader of `case-retention-days`) joins `runRetention`, and `RetentionReport` gains `redactedCases`, `failedCaseRedactions` and `deletedUserNotifications`.
16. **The seed.** `seedCaseWorkflows(db, environmentId, seededBy)` (`seed/case-workflows.ts`, `api-case-workflows`) runs in `seed/dev-seed.ts` after `seedWorkflowRecipes`.
17. **Answers the plan leaves open.** `PATCH /cases/:id` takes up to 14 MiB (C135.1). `POST /cases`, a template, a version, a case action and a review request answer 201; `POST /cases/bulk` 202; `run-action` 200. `GET /case-actions` needs `workflows:write`, as account actions do. `POST /case-comments/:id/redact` needs `cases:write`, and the service lets the author through and anyone else with `cases:redact` (C134.2). The routes are in `cases.ts`, `case-templates.ts`, `case-comments.ts` (`api-cases`), `case-actions.ts`, `inquiry-review-requests.ts` (`api-case-workflows`), `case-print.ts` (`api-case-files`) and `routes/dashboard/notifications.ts`, with their services in the owners' paths; `case-routes.test.ts` holds each route to 501 and its scope until built.
18. **`events.caused_by_bulk_id` reads `cblk_`** (`events_caused_by_bulk_id_format`), so the three tests that wrote `bulk-1` write a `cblk_` id.
19. **Existing tests changed with the plan,** none weakened: `testing.test.ts` (the table list), `schema-v2.test.ts` (the sealed columns), `key-rotation.test.ts` (the seeds), `subject-ids.test.ts` (`case`, `cscm`), `workflow-plumbing.test.ts`, `events/publisher-workflows.test.ts` and `workflows/engine/waits.test.ts` (a `cblk_` bulk id), `services/redaction.test.ts` (`CASE_PII_TABLES`), `services/retention.test.ts` and `retention-run.test.ts` (the three report members), `services/context.test.ts` (`caseBulk`), and the `RedactionService` fakes of `jobs/retention.test.ts`, `retention-run.test.ts`, `services/graph-hooks.test.ts`, `services/retention.test.ts`, `services/transaction-hooks.test.ts` and `test-support/fakes.ts` (`redactCase`).
20. **A load-sensitive integration test, fixed.** `src/webhooks/queue.integration.test.ts` waited out its 60 s once in an integration shard at a load of about 15: its `QueueEvents` read the stream from `$`, after `waitUntilReady`, so a job that failed before the first read was never seen. It reads from `0-0` now (`c85389bc`), which no timing defeats.
21. **PGlite.** Cold first database 4,592 to 4,622 ms against 4,641 to 4,940 ms before, warm 1,069 to 1,156 ms against 985 to 1,023 ms, at loads of 1.1 to 4.8: the 18 tables cost nothing a hook would notice.

### Checkpoint (Q) queues, assignment and SLA

22. **Tables.** `case_queues` (`cqueue_`), `case_queue_members`, `case_assignment_policies` (`cap_`), `case_assignment_policy_sources`, `case_sla_policies` (`cslap_`) and `case_calendars` (`ccal_`), in `packages/db/src/tables/case-queues.ts`, except the sources, which sit in `cases.ts` because they name templates. Every reference between them, and from a case or a version to them, is a composite foreign key to `(environment_id, id)`, as checkpoint (C)'s are: `cases_queue_fk`, `cases_sla_policy_fk`, `cases_sla_calendar_fk`, `case_template_versions_default_queue_fk`, `_assignment_policy_fk`, `_sla_policy_fk`, `case_queues_policy_fk`, `case_sla_policies_calendar_fk`, `case_assignment_policy_sources_template_fk` and `_queue_fk`. None cascades but a member's from its queue and a source's from its policy, because a queue, a policy and a calendar have no delete route (C129.5): only an environment's deletion removes them.
23. **Members.** One row per user and queue (`case_queue_members_pkey`), `max_open` 1 to 500 (default 50), `paused` (default false), a foreign key to `users` that cascades, so a deleted user leaves every queue. The 200-member cap and "a member holds `cases:write`" are the service's (`case_assignee_ineligible`). Deactivation or a lost permission does not delete the row: the `case-reassign` sweep skips such a member and returns their cases (C132.6), and `api-queues-sla` decides whether it also prunes the row. (Default.)
24. **Policies.** `agent_ids` is a `text[]` of 1 to 200 `usr_` ids, `null` meaning the routed queue's members, as the template's `notify_on_create` and a comment's `mentions` are; a user belongs to an organisation, not an environment, so a foreign key would not hold anything an array cannot. `max_capacity` is 1 to 500 or `null` (each member's `max_open`). The sources are rows, in `position` order from 0, each exactly one template or queue of the policy's environment (`num_nonnulls`), each once, at most 50 (`policy-sources`).
25. **The round-robin cursor (C132.3).** `case_queues.round_robin_cursor` is the last member a queue gave a case to; `case_assignment_policies.round_robin_cursor` serves a case that enters no queue (created on a template whose version names a policy and no queue). The service locks the row whose cursor it moves. (Default.)
26. **A case's SLA (C133.4).** `cases` gains `sla_state` (`none` by default), `sla_epoch`, `sla_consumed_ms`, `sla_segment_started_at`, `sla_expires_at`, `sla_expired_at`, `sla_met_at`, and what the SLA counts to: `sla_target_ms` (business milliseconds: a policy's target, or the public `sla-expires-in-seconds`, which counts on the `24x7` calendar), `sla_policy_id` with `sla_policy_revision`, `sla_case_calendar_id` and `sla_on_reopen` (`continue` by default). The checks: `sla_expires_at` is null exactly while the state is `none` or `paused`, which is the contract's rule (Stage 1a amendment 37); a target exactly while there is an SLA; `met` and `expired` with their instants; the policy pair whole; a calendar only beside a policy; counts non-negative and a revision from 1. The column shapes follow `case-logic`'s `CaseSla` and `SlaTarget` (`services/cases/sla/sla-state.ts`).
27. **"A case pins the revision its SLA started under" (Stage 1a amendment 34).** There is no revisions table: the case copies what it pins (the target, the calendar and `on_reopen`) beside the policy id and revision, so a later edit moves only cases that start after it, and the sweep and a recompute read the case alone. A calendar edit still moves every running or paused SLA on that calendar (decision 59), found through `cases_sla_calendar_idx`. (Default.)
28. **Indexes.** `cases_pull_idx` `(environment_id, case_queue_id, sla_expires_at, created_at, id) WHERE assignee_id IS NULL AND status_category <> 'end' AND redacted_at IS NULL`, C132.3's with `assignee_id` for its `assignee_user_id` (amendment 2); `api-queues-sla` owns the `EXPLAIN` test. `cases_open_assignee_idx` `(environment_id, assignee_id, case_queue_id)` over open, unredacted, assigned cases, for capacity, fewest-open and the re-assignment. `cases_sla_due_idx` `(sla_expires_at) WHERE sla_state = 'running'`, across environments, for the sweep's batches of 500. `cases_sla_calendar_idx` (decision 59). `case_queue_members_user_idx` (a person's queues). `case_sla_policies_calendar_idx`. The two source indexes.
29. **Repository stubs and seeds.** `api-queues-sla` owns `repositories/case-queues.ts` (`getCaseQueueRow`, `lockCaseQueueRow`, `listCaseQueueMembers`, `pullNextCaseId`), `case-policies.ts` (`getCaseAssignmentPolicyRow`, `listCaseAssignmentPolicySources`, `getCaseSlaPolicyRow`, `getCaseCalendarRow`) and `case-sla.ts` (`DueCaseSla`, `lockDueCaseSlas`), each rejecting with `CaseRepositoryNotBuiltError`, exported whole from the repository index, so a name the owner adds is exported too. Seeds by SQL in `@openverify/db/testing`: `seedCaseQueue` (with members), `seedCaseAssignmentPolicy` (with sources in order), `seedCaseCalendar` (nine to five on weekdays in Europe/London), `seedCaseSlaPolicy` (240 minutes, around the clock); `seedCase` takes `caseQueueId` and `assigneeId` (a self-assignment by that user).
30. **The sweeps (C143.1).** `case-sla` every 30 s and `case-reassign` every 60 s (`jobs/case-sweep-queues.ts`), registered by `caseRepeatableJobs(deps, { events })` (`jobs/repeatable/index.ts`) whenever the worker runs the case jobs (`options.cases`, which `main` passes). Each calls `runCaseSlaSweep` or `runCaseReassignSweep` (`jobs/repeatable/cases.ts`, `api-queues-sla`) with `CaseSweepDeps { db, clock, logger, events }`; the stubs find nothing to do. No mailer is passed: the breach notification's email (C133.7, C134.4) needs one, and `api-queues-sla` or `api-cases` names it as a deviation with the change to `worker.ts`.
31. **The routes.** `case-queues.ts` (`GET`, `POST /case-queues`; `GET`, `PATCH /case-queues/:id`; `PUT /case-queues/:id/members`), `case-assignment.ts` (`POST /cases/:id/assign`, `POST /cases/next`), `case-assignment-policies.ts`, `case-sla-policies.ts` and `case-calendars.ts` (with `POST /case-calendars/:id/holidays/import`), each answering 501 through its service stub in `services/cases/{queues/service,assignment/service,assignment/policies,sla/policies,sla/calendars}.ts`. Reads take `case-queues:read` and writes `case-queues:write`. `assign` and `next` take `cases:write`, because a route's scopes are all required: the service asks `cases:assign` to assign anyone but the caller (C129.5). Creates answer 201, the rest 200. `case-queue-routes.test.ts` holds each to 501, its scope and its frozen body. `POST /cases/next` is a C129.4 exit: its service filters by template visibility.
32. **Masking (Stage 1a amendment 38).** `user-email-address` joins the stored log's `MASKED_MEMBERS`, because it sits in `meta`; it was already in `MASKED_ATTRIBUTES`, the log's attribute set and `MASKED_ATTRIBUTE_NAMES` through `PII_CASE_ATTRIBUTES`. A holiday import's `csv` is masked whole already (`PII_WORKFLOW_RUN_ATTRIBUTES`). No (Q) column is sealed, so the rewrap, redaction and rebuild lists are unchanged.
33. **PGlite.** Cold first database 4,959 to 4,999 ms (one run 9,029 ms as the load rose to 5.8) against 4,762 to 4,947 ms before; warm 1,252 to 1,370 ms against 1,083 to 1,135 ms; loads 1.8 to 5.8. The 120 s hook budget is untouched.

### Checkpoint (F) files, renditions and exports

34. **Four tables, one beyond the (F) list.** `case_attachments` (`catt_`), `file_renditions`, `rendition_pages` and `case_exports` (`cexp_`), in `packages/db/src/tables/case-files.ts`. `rendition_pages` holds the sealed page images of either source, an inquiry's `files` row or an attachment, keyed `<source id>/<page>` (Stage 1a amendment 42: a rendition has no prefix of its own; the key is what `kek:rewrap` pages by). A rendition's state lives with its source: an attachment's on `case_attachments` (`rendition_status`, `rendition_page_count`, `rendition_truncated`, `rendition_error_code`, `rendition_attempts`, `rendered_at`), which the contract serves, and an inquiry file's in `file_renditions` (`status`, `page_count`, `truncated`, `error_code`, `attempts`, `rendered_at`).
35. **The foreign key on `files(id)` (Stage 1a amendment 9).** (Default.) `file_renditions.file_id` references `files(id)` and cascades: the state goes with its file. `rendition_pages` has no foreign key on its source, so a page outlives a `files` row deleted outright and its object is still found; a cascading page row would have taken its wrapped key and left the object in storage for nothing to find. Every path that redacts or deletes a `files` row calls `deleteRenditionsForFilesInTx(tx, { environmentId, fileIds })` (`services/cases/attachments/renditions.ts`, `api-case-files`) in its transaction and deletes the keys it answers after the commit with the objects it already deletes: the inquiry's redaction (`services/redaction.ts`), a child inquiry's (`workflows/actions/transactions/children.ts`), `POST /transactions/:id/redact-biometrics` (`services/transactions/transactions.ts`), a rejected handoff (`services/handoff/flow-handoffs.ts`), a replaced flow upload (`services/flow.ts`), a replaced server-written file (`services/server-files.ts`, through `replacedStorageKeys`) and a discarded selfie capture (`services/selfie-captures.ts`, through `storageKeys`). The owner decides whether the hook deletes the page rows or marks them `discarded_at`; `purgeDiscardedRenditions` (the C16 pass) deletes the objects of discarded pages and of pages whose source row is gone, then the rows.
36. **`case_attachments`.** The object's `storage_key` and `wrapped_dek` are whole and present exactly until `deleted_at`, which needs `redacted_at`; the sealed name and the digest are kept until `redacted_at` (P34: a redacted row keeps its id, type, size and scan status). `mime_type` is one of the four `CASE_ATTACHMENT_MEDIA_TYPES`, at most 10 MiB. The contract's three rules are CHECKs: a scan names its provider and `not_scanned` none (H20); a verdict (`clean`, `infected`) has its `scanned_at` and nothing else but an `error` does; a ready rendition has its page count (1 to 20) and nothing else does. `scan_attempts` is 0 to 3. The uploader is `user`, `api-key` or `workflow-run`. Indexes: a case's attachments in upload order, the pending scans, and the redacted objects not yet deleted.
37. **`case_exports`.** Its creator is a user or a key (a run never exports). The request (a filter or a search, which can name a field's value) is sealed for the row and kept only while `pending` or `running`; a finished export names no filter (P19). Its file's key and wrapped key are set exactly while `completed`, with a byte size; `completed` and `expired` have their count, completion and expiry; a failure has its code and nothing else does. Indexes: the creator's exports newest first (P26), the completed ones by expiry (`case-export-sweep`) and the unfinished ones.
38. **Sealing, rewrap and subjects (C143.5).** `ENCRYPTED_COLUMNS` gains `case_attachments.original_filename` (row), `case_attachments.wrapped_dek`, `case_exports.request` (row), `case_exports.wrapped_dek` and `rendition_pages.wrapped_dek` (each `file`); `SEALED` gains `caseAttachmentFilename` and `caseExportRequest`; `REWRAP_TABLES` gains the three tables; `REDACTION_SUBJECT_ID_PREFIXES` gains `catt`. `CASE_PII_TABLES` (`services/redaction.test-support.ts`) stands the three aside from the subject scan, because none can exist until `api-case-files` builds them; its suite owes the coverage. An export is not redacted with its cases: its file is gone after 24 hours. (Default.)
39. **Repository stubs and seeds.** `api-case-files` owns `repositories/case-attachments.ts` (`createCaseAttachmentRow`, `getCaseAttachmentRow`, `listCaseAttachments`), `file-renditions.ts` (`getFileRendition`, `listRenditionPages`, `discardRenditionPagesInTx`) and `case-exports.ts` (`createCaseExportRow`, `getCaseExportRow`, `listCaseExports`, `CaseExportCreator`), each rejecting with `CaseRepositoryNotBuiltError` and exported whole from the index. Seeds by SQL in `@openverify/db/testing`: `seedCaseAttachment` (not scanned, pending), `seedFileRendition`, `seedRenditionPage` (answers `<source>/<page>`), `seedCaseExport` (pending CSV by a user).
40. **The scanner (C135.5, decision 66; Stage 1b (X) amendment 43).** `AttachmentScanner` (`ports.ts`) has `provider` and `scan({ attachmentId, bytes })`, answering `{ verdict: 'clean' | 'infected' | 'error', provider }`; `BoundProviders` gains `attachmentScanner`. `createAttachmentScanner(provider, { clamdSocket })` (`providers/attachment-scanner/index.ts`, `api-case-files`) is bound in the worker for `sandbox` and `clamd`; `none` and unset bind nothing, so every attachment is `not_scanned`. `clamd` without `OPENVERIFY_CLAMD_SOCKET` refuses to bind. The stubs' `scan` rejects with 501, so nothing reads as scanned before the owner builds it. The API process binds no scanner, and reads a fact by name, as `slackBound` is read: `config.cases.attachmentScanners` (`{ sandbox, production }`, each `sandbox`, `clamd` or `null`) from `OPENVERIFY_PROVIDER_ATTACHMENT_SCANNER_{SANDBOX,PRODUCTION}`, refusing a name it does not know and the sandbox scanner for production without quoting the value, and `ApiDependencies.attachmentScanners` carries it. **For `integration-8b`:** compose must pass these two names to the API, and `infra/tests/compose.test.sh`'s check `api receives no scanner setting` must narrow to `^OPENVERIFY_CLAMD` (the socket stays the worker's); until then the API in the compose stack reads no scanner and serves as `none`.
41. **The queues (C143.2).** `jobs/case-file-queues.ts`: `case-export` (`createCaseExportQueue`, job id the `cexp_` id), `case-attachment-scan` (`createCaseAttachmentScanQueue`, the `catt_` id) and `case-rendition` (`createCaseRenditionQueue`, the source's `file_` or `catt_` id), each carrying ids alone, refusing anything else, removed on completion and failure. `ApiDependencies` gains `caseExports`, `caseAttachmentScans` and `caseRenditions`, which the container builds and closes, and `DashboardContext` (and `dashboardRoutes`' options) gains `caseRenditions`, for an inquiry file's first view. The worker's case options (`WorkerCaseOptions`) gain `renditions` and the processors `export`, `scan` and `rendition`; `startWorkers` starts the three workers with `CaseFileJobDeps { db, storage, keyring, vision, events, clock, logger, providers }`, each calling its processor in `jobs/cases/index.ts` (`api-case-files`), stubs that find nothing to do; `main` passes the `case-rendition` producer. `case-export-sweep` (60 s, `CASE_EXPORT_SWEEP_QUEUE` in `jobs/case-sweep-queues.ts`) joins `caseRepeatableJobs`, whose deps gain `storage`, and calls `runCaseExportSweep({ db, storage, clock, logger })` (`jobs/cases/export-sweep.ts`).
42. **`requestRenditionsForFiles` (V27, inventory 13.1).** `jobs/process-verification.ts` calls it after `typeJob.afterCommit`, with the verification's files read again (a decision may have written a preview), through `ProcessVerificationDeps.renditions`, which the worker passes. A rejection is logged (`renditions could not be requested`, with the error's name) and fails nothing.
43. **The C16 pass.** `purgeDiscardedRenditions` runs in `runRetention` beside `purgeRedactedFiles`, not inside it, with its own count `purgedRenditionPages`, so `purgedFiles` stays a count of files; `runCaseExportRetention` (`services/cases/exports/retention.ts`) runs after the case steps. `RetentionReport` gains `purgedRenditionPages`, `expiredCaseExports` and `failedCaseExportDeletes`.
44. **Masking (Stage 1a amendments 39 and 40).** The stored log nulls a PATCH item's `data` and `filename` inside an `attachments` array alone (`CASE_ATTACHMENT_BODY_PII_MEMBERS`), so `data` elsewhere stays; `filename` was masked at any depth already. `PiiMask.attributes` nulls each attachment's `filename` for a reader without `pii:view` (`auth/pii.ts`), so every serializer that masks a case's attributes masks it. `PII_CASE_ATTACHMENT_ATTRIBUTES` joins `MASKED_ATTRIBUTES`, the log's attribute set and `MASKED_ATTRIBUTE_NAMES`; in the trigger view the nested `filename` is PII through the contract's `.meta({ pii: true })`, which a test reads from `triggerViewType('case')`.
45. **The hook for case redaction (C139.1), for `api-cases`.** `redactCaseAttachmentsInTx(tx, { environmentId, caseIds, at })` (`services/cases/attachments/redaction.ts`, `api-case-files`) answers `{ storageKeys }`: the originals' and their pages' objects, which the caller deletes after its commit. Its stub finds nothing, because no attachment can exist yet. The exact change for `api-cases`: in `redactCasesInTx` (`services/cases/redaction.ts`), on a first redaction, call it in the case's transaction and add its `storageKeys` to the answer's `storageKeys`.
46. **The routes.** `routes/server/case-attachments.ts` (`caseAttachmentRoutes`): `POST /cases/:id/attachments` under `cases:write`, one multipart file, **buffered** under the plugin's own `multipart/form-data` parser at `CASE_ATTACHMENT_BODY_LIMIT_BYTES` (10 MiB plus 64 KiB of framing), because the idempotency plugin refuses a key on a streamed body and C135.1 honours it as for buffered bodies; answers 201 with the case. `GET /cases/:id/attachments/:attachmentId/content` under `cases:read` (`pii:view` in the service), `?page` 1 to 20, 200 an image as an attachment named by the server with `nosniff` and `private, no-store`, 202 `renditionPendingDocumentSchema` with `Retry-After`; `…/original` under `cases:redact` (`pii:view` in the service). `routes/server/case-exports.ts` (`caseExportRoutes`): `POST /case-exports` answers **202** with the export (asynchronous, as `POST /cases/bulk`), `GET /case-exports`, `GET /case-exports/:id` and `GET /case-exports/:id/download` (CSV or JSON as an attachment), all under `cases:export`. `routes/dashboard/file-renditions.ts` (`fileRenditionRoutes`): `GET /api/v1/dashboard/files/:id/rendition` under `inquiries:read` and `pii:view`, `?page`, 200 an image, 202 pending. Their services answer 501: `services/cases/attachments/service.ts` (`uploadCaseAttachment`, `getCaseAttachmentContent`, `getCaseAttachmentOriginal`, `CaseAttachmentUpload { body, contentType }`, `CaseAttachmentContent`), `services/cases/exports/service.ts` (`createCaseExport`, `listCaseExports`, `getCaseExport`, `downloadCaseExport`, `CaseExportFile`) and `services/cases/attachments/file-renditions.ts` (`getFileRenditionContent`). `case-print.ts` was frozen with (C). `docs/api/openapi.json` is regenerated after this last checkpoint, two writes identical. The `case-attachments` and `case-exports` rate limits are the services' to apply, with a test of their fail mode.
47. **Not covered by a join test, named so it is not lost.** The rendition hook at a replaced flow upload, at `redact-biometrics` and at a rejected handoff is called in each transaction, and the existing suites of those three paths pass, but no test replaces the hook there as `case-file-joins.test.ts` does for the inquiry's and a child's redaction, a replaced server file and a discarded capture. `api-case-files` proves each once the hook deletes pages. And a process note: `.prettierignore`'s `services/` also matches `apps/api/src/services/`, so nothing there is format-checked and many of its files are not prettier-formatted; run prettier on named files outside it only (the TDD file records a slip this caused, undone before any commit).
48. **PGlite.** Before (a private worktree at `e80f0a90`), cold first database 5,000 to 6,589 ms and warm 1,903 to 2,401 ms at loads of 10.2 to 12.4; after, cold 9,834 to 10,422 ms and warm 2,343 to 2,611 ms at 11.4 to 13.5; interleaved at the same load (13 to 15), before 11,904 and 10,545 ms, after 11,333 and 9,028 ms. The difference is the load, not the four tables. The 120 s hook budget is untouched.

## Workflow 8B Stage 1a amendments (contracts-8b, call 2)

Recorded by `integration-8b` from `contracts-8b`'s second report, which numbered them 32 to 45 and did not write them here. The evidence is `docs/tdd/phase8-contracts-8b.tdd.md`, "Call 2 of 2". Items marked (default) are on the owner's review list.

32. **Node groups.** The plan's one `casePolicies` group is two, `caseAssignmentPolicies` and `caseSlaPolicies`, as every route family has its own.
33. **Queues (default).** A queue's "strategy" (C129.3) is its nullable `case-assignment-policy-id`. Archiving is `PATCH` with `status: archived`, permanent. `PUT /case-queues/:id/members` replaces the list (`max-open` 50, `paused` false by default), at most 200 members, each once.
34. **SLA policies (default).** A policy does not repeat `starts-on` or the pause categories: they are the version's `sla-starts-on` and each status's `pauses-sla`. `case-calendar-id: null` is 24x7; `revision` counts edits from 1, and a case pins the revision it started under; `on-reopen` defaults to `continue`.
35. **Assignment policies (default).** `agents: null` is the routed queue's members; `max-capacity: null` is each member's `max-open`; `sources` are 1 to 50 templates or queues in order; `sort` defaults to `created-at`. Policies and calendars have no status and no delete route.
36. **Calendars.** The contract checks the shape (in-order `[start, end)` `HH:MM` intervals, at most 8 a day, `24:00` as an end, 60 business minutes a week at least, real holiday dates once each, local closure pairs, an IANA-shaped zone); the service checks the zone and the two-year boundaries (`calendar_invalid`). The holiday import is `data.attributes.csv` (PII, 65,536 characters) with `replace`; its answer lists refusals by row and code.
37. **A case's SLA.** `sla-state` is served; `sla-expires-at` is null exactly while `none` or `paused`, and `met` and `expired` keep the deadline.
38. **Bodies.** `assign` takes `meta.user-id` (null unassigns) or `meta.user-email-address` (PII), one of them, and `lock-version`; `next` takes `meta.case-queue-ids` (1 to 50) and answers `{ data: case | null }`. `user-email-address` joins `PII_CASE_ATTRIBUTES` and the stored log's `MASKED_MEMBERS`.
39. **Attachments.** The served `attachments[]` members, with the contract's rules: a verdict needs a provider and `not_scanned` none (H20); `scanned-at` with a verdict or `error`; `page-count` exactly when the rendition is ready; at most 20 a case.
40. **PII.** `PII_CASE_ATTACHMENT_ATTRIBUTES` is `filename`; a PATCH item's `data` and `filename` are masked inside an `attachments` array only; an event's case names no filename (`carriesNoValue`).
41. **PATCH uploads (H31).** 1 to 20 items, each and all together at most 10 MiB decoded, checked from base64; the 14 MiB body limit is the route's.
42. **Renditions (default).** `page` 1 to 20; pending answers 202 with `retry-after-seconds` (5 s) and `Retry-After`; a rendition vision could not make answers 422 `rendition_unavailable` (ours); a deleted or redacted source 404. A rendition has no id prefix: its key is the source's id and page.
43. **Vision `POST /v1/renditions` (F36).** One multipart `source`; the answer's shape and both sides' checks (pages from 1, at most 20, `truncated` above 20, images at most 4,096 px a side, PDF pages PNG at most 1,600 px, at most 16 MiB a page, IHDR and SOI checked). `VisionClient.createRendition`. Amended by Stage 2 item 56 for PDFs above 50 pages.
44. **Exports (default).** `format`, `include-personal-data`, at most one of `filter` and `search`, neither meaning every visible case. The resource names no filter or value (P19). `CASE_EXPORT_CSV_COLUMNS`, with each held kind's ids in one cell and `fields` only with personal data; `caseExportBundleSchema` for JSON.
45. **Node.** `cases` gains `uploadAttachment`, `attachmentContent` and `attachmentOriginal` (raw answers); `GetCaseQuery` is a type.

## Workflow 8B Stage 2 amendments

Recorded by `integration-8b` from the reports of `case-logic` and `vision-renditions`. Evidence: `docs/tdd/phase8-case-logic.tdd.md` and `phase8-vision-renditions.tdd.md`.

### case-logic

49. **Shared file.** Three export lines in `packages/core/src/cases/index.ts`, which its header reserved for this agent.
50. **F30.** The flow bundle-size assertion is a module-graph test (`packages/core/src/cases/temporal-boundary.test.ts`): core declares and imports no Temporal package, no workspace package the flow reaches declares one, and only the API and the engine declare `temporal-polyfill`. A measured gzip budget of the built flow is carried (Stage 4 item 106).
51. **The status machine (C131.1; default).** A move between two `end` statuses re-resolves without reopening; a reopen stages `case.status-updated` then `case.reopened`; a status the map omits has no exits; a resolution outside `end` or outside `resolutions`, and a status tag the entered status does not list, are refused; a whitespace comment is missing; the same status is `no_change` whatever `meta` holds; checks run status, map, resolution, tag, comment. The publish codes are `initial_status_count`, `end_status_missing`, `status_repeated`, `transition_status_unknown`, `transition_to_itself`, `transition_repeated`, `end_status_unreachable`, each with a pointer, rendered by `api-cases` in a 422 (no named problem).
52. **Search (C137.2; default).** `template` and `case_queue` compare names, `state` is the category, `tag_id` a tag name; `eq null` on the nullable attributes; comparisons on the four timestamps only, RFC 3339 with an offset; `assignee` takes `usr_`, `resolver` `usr_`, `key_` or `wfr_`; a `fields.*` leaf is equality on a string of at most 1,024 characters, a number or a boolean. Seven `search_*` codes with pointers, at most 20, no value.
53. **Assignment (C132.2, C132.3; default).** Capacity is the lower of `max-open` and `max-capacity`; a listed non-member has the default 50; the creator strategies assign only an eligible agent; user ids in code-unit order; round-robin keeps its cursor when nobody is eligible; manual assignment checks active, `cases:write` and visibility only.
54. **Business time (C133.3).** The zone's transitions are listed once through Temporal and local times resolved from them (`compatible` and `reject` as Temporal), held to Temporal's own answers by a differential test of 7 zones; `null` is 24x7; scans of at most 3,661 days (`business_time_scan_exceeded`); a zero target answers the start; validation over 730 days from today's local date, `24:00` named by the next day.
55. **SLA transitions (C133.4, C133.5; default).** A public seconds SLA is a 24x7 target that pauses; a paused SLA resolved is `met` with its would-be deadline; resolving past an unswept deadline is `expired` without firing; pausing past one expires and fires; an expiry keeps the epoch, every other change moves it; `recomputeSla` runs the remainder on the new calendar; reopening an expired SLA under `continue` keeps it expired. `expireSla(sla, target, at)` takes the target.

### vision-renditions

56. **Above 50 pages (amends item 43; default).** A PDF of 21 to 50 pages is rendered to 20 and marked truncated; one of more than 50 is refused 422 `too_many_pages`, because the guarded child refuses it before rendering, and `api-case-files` reads that as `rendition_unavailable`.
57. **`app.py`.** Beside the three named edits, `renditions` joins the existing `from openverify_vision import ...` line.
58. **The rendition rules (default).** A JPEG gives a JPEG at quality 90; a PNG or WebP a PNG, or a JPEG (90, 75, 50) past 16 MiB, else 413; transparency on white; 16-bit grey scaled; the ICC profile dropped, never converted (no littlecms on an untrusted profile); an animation's first frame; 4,096 px a side after orientation.
59. **Deadline and lane (provisional).** 40 s from arrival, queueing included; each page's child gets what is left, at most 30 s; past it 503 `guarded_worker_failed`. The lane is one running and four queued, a sixth answered 503 busy with `Retry-After`. The API waits its guarded 45 s for every rendition.
60. **Refusals.** `pdf_encrypted`, `pdf_unprocessable` and `too_many_pages` are coded 422s; the guard's format, size and corruption answers are 415, 413 and 422; a child failure or the deadline is 503. A 4xx means the rendition failed; a 5xx is retried.

## Workflow 8B Stage 3 amendments

Recorded by `integration-8b` from the four Stage 3 reports. Evidence: `docs/tdd/phase8-api-cases.tdd.md`, `phase8-api-queues-sla.tdd.md`, `phase8-api-case-files.tdd.md`, `phase8-api-case-workflows.tdd.md`. Where Stage 4 changed a default, its item says so.

### api-cases

61. **Template visibility (C129.4; default).** A key without `case-template-ids` sees only templates listing no role; a user sees a restricted one through a listed role; the system sees every template; an id naming nothing hides nothing. `GET /workflow-trigger-deliveries` is not filtered: a delivery names no case, and its run is filtered at its own route. A key's restriction is refused by pointer when an id is not a template the creator sees, never saying which.
62. **Creation and comments (default).** `creator-email-address` resolves to a user of the caller's organisation, who becomes the creator; an address naming nobody is a 422 by pointer. Mention tokens count only for an active, `cases:read`, template-visible user of the organisation, at most 50; others stay text; the author is not notified. Reopening clears the resolver too. A comment sent with `set-status` becomes a case comment.
63. **Publish rules.** A 422 whose `errors[]` carry the field-schema and transition-map codes, `field_type_changed` (checked against every earlier non-draft version) and an unreachable `notify-on-create` user, each with a pointer; no named problem was added.
64. **Search and bulk (default).** Search problems are a 422 with `search_*` codes; a `fields.*` query without PII visibility is 403 naming `pii:view`; `links.next` and `links.prev` are case ids; a missing timestamp sorts last; default page 10; `case-search` keyed by the principal. Bulk `case-ids` are checked by the job under the creator's visibility (`not_found` when hidden); a search selection is resolved when asked; a refusal's code is the problem's, else `not_found`, `forbidden`, `conflict`, `gone` or `unprocessable` by status; bulk assign follows the manual rule with strategy `manual`. The job's events carry the `cblk_` id and the creator as principal, never a person's step.
65. **Public SLA.** `setPublicSlaInTx` replaces a policy's SLA (policy, revision and calendar nulled, `on-reopen` continue); clearing gives no SLA in a new epoch; a `PATCH` change fires `case.updated`. On create the public SLA starts before `assignOnEntry`.
66. **Redaction and retention (C139.1, C139.3).** A principal's redaction is audited from inside `redactCasesInTx` (no request context); a cascade or retention writes a system entry naming its cause; a `redacted` history row; tags cleared, resolution and statuses kept. `redactCaseCopiesInTx` empties a case target's fields and deletes its keys, rather than redacting the whole case, because the case's other content is not the subject's (default). Retention redacts only in a pass that has a publisher; each case in its own transaction. Notifications are kept 90 days (provisional).
67. **Widened while nothing called them.** `createCaseRow`, `getCaseRow` and `lockCaseRow` answer `Case` (fields opened); `createCaseCommentRow` answers `CaseComment`; `redactCaseCommentRows` takes `at`; `getCaseBulkActionRow` answers its record; `CaseBulkDeps` gains `events?`; `CaseCreationInput` gains `acting?` (without it a `platform-file` field is refused). `CaseProblemError` lives in `services/cases/problems.ts`.
68. **`GET /cases/:id?include=`** serves `case-template` and `case-comments` (the newest 50, masked); `case-queue` and the seven held kinds answer 501 naming them (carried, Stage 4 item 102).
69. **Existing tests changed with the plan:** `case-plumbing.test.ts` (db and API), `routes/dashboard/notifications.test.ts`, `routes/server/case-routes.test.ts` (29 routes built), `routes/graph-query-routes.test.ts` (404 for an unknown template).

### api-queues-sla

70. **`pullNextCaseId(tx, env, queueIds, reader)`** takes the reader, so the pull applies template visibility (a C129.4 exit). `case-queue-plumbing.test.ts` and `case-queue-routes.test.ts` changed with the plan (no 501, scopes and schemas kept).
71. **When a policy SLA starts (default).** Inside `assignOnEntry` (`created`, `entered-queue`) and every assignment (`assigned`), only on a case that never had an SLA, so a public deadline or a clear wins.
72. **The status hook's events.** When `applySlaOnStatusChangeInTx` fires on an unresolved case (a pause past an unswept deadline), it stages `case.sla-expired` through `createOutboxOnlyPublisher(tx)` in the status change's transaction, and the outbox sweeps deliver it. Its history rows are `system`. Widening `CaseStatusChange` with the caller's publisher is carried (Stage 4 item 104).
73. **Members (default).** Replacing a queue's members returns their open cases of that queue in the same transaction (unassigned with reason `member-left`, offered to the policy again), because the sweep cannot tell a member who left from a non-member someone assigned by hand. The reassign sweep keeps the member rows of a deactivated user (Stage 1b item 23, decided) and never chooses them.
74. **Assignment (default).** Open counts span the environment (decision 67); a queue without a policy falls back to its version's, with the queue's members as agents; an assigned case is never reassigned on entry; an archived queue assigns nobody; the pull ignores capacity and pause and is a user's alone (a key gets 403); `inquiry-creator` reads the user actor of the first held inquiry's `inquiry.created` audit entry, because inquiries have no creator column. Without `cases:assign`, assigning or unassigning anyone but oneself is 403 before any lookup; the same assignee again is no change.
75. **SLA policies and calendars (default).** `revision` moves for `target-minutes`, `case-calendar-id` and `on-reopen`, not for a name; a target the calendar cannot reach within ten years is `sla_out_of_range` at policy writes and at a calendar edit that would strand a running SLA; a zone the runtime forgets is `calendar_invalid`; an import is an edit and recomputes; stored holidays are sorted; row codes `invalid_value`, `date_repeated` and the CSV reader's; the header is `date,label`.
76. **No breach email.** The `case-sla` sweep writes the in-app `sla-breached` notification; no mailer reaches the sweeps (carried, Stage 4 item 103). The cursor write moves the queue's (or policy's) `updated-at`. `sort` and `sources` are stored and served, unread by any drain job (carried, Stage 4 item 105).
77. **Lock order (owner review list).** Members-set and archiving lock the queue, then its cases; entry, the pull and the reassign sweep lock a case, then its queue. A removed member who is also stranded can deadlock the two; Postgres aborts one, the sweep retries next pass, the route answers 500.
78. **TDD process.** `case-pull.integration.test.ts`, `queues-races.integration.test.ts`, `queues/api-logs.test.ts` and `queues/audit.test.ts` were written after their slices' GREEN and passed at once; recorded, none weakened.

### api-case-files

79. **The PATCH store (F15).** `acceptPatchAttachments(tx, target, attachments, store?)` takes a `PatchAttachmentStore { deps, environmentKind, requestId? }`; without it any attachment still answers 501. `api-cases`' `updateCase` passes it since Stage 4 (item 92).
80. **Serving and scanning (default).** A scan that ended in `error` answers a plain 409 with no code (a later contracts change could add `attachment_scan_failed`); the original route applies the same scanning rule, so an infected or unscannable original is not served even to `cases:redact`. A worker that has not bound the provider the API recorded leaves the attachment `pending` and logs it. Scans try three times in one run; a retryable vision failure leaves the rendition `pending`, and every content request that finds it pending enqueues it again; a non-retryable answer records `failed` (`rendition_unavailable`); infected or unscannable attachments are never rendered.
81. **Exports (default).** The selection is decided in the API when asked (a field search needs the blind-index key, which the job has not), sealed as `{ case-ids }`, at most 10,000; the job re-reads them under the creator's visibility; a finished export drops the request. Before completion a download is 404, after expiry 410; a failed job records `export_failed`; JSON without personal data is fully masked; a redacted case reads `redacted` in the CSV's `fields` and `tags`; file names `case-export-<id>.csv|json`.
82. **Events and history (C131.5).** A multipart upload stages `case.updated` and writes `attachment-added`; a scan writes `attachment-scanned` (system), an export `exported` per case, print `print-initiated`. A rendition page is sealed with its `<source>/<page>` id as context; pages are discarded (`discarded_at`), a file's rendition state reads `deleted`, and `purgeDiscardedRenditions` also deletes the objects of redacted attachments left undeleted. Only image and PDF files get renditions, never chip files. An object put for a PATCH whose transaction rolls back stays in storage unreadable.
83. **TDD process.** The inquiry-file rendition service was written before its test and moved aside so RED ran on the stub; the purge and request hooks were built before their own tests; a RED that typechecked under lint but not under `tsc` was fixed test-only in `8f27d8d1`. Existing tests changed with the plan: `case-file-plumbing.test.ts` (db and API), `phase8-providers.test.ts`, `case-file-routes.test.ts`, `case-routes.test.ts` (print built), `file-renditions.test.ts`.

### api-case-workflows

84. **The case view.** A case's `fetch_object` answers the flattened view of the masked case (`buildTriggerView`), the shape `triggerViewType('case')` types, so `steps.x.output.object.resolution` reads the stored value. `caseResourceOf` reads `api-cases`' builder, so a run's view and `GET /cases/:id` cannot drift. `current_case` is typed for a `trigger-case`, an event trigger on case or case-comment events only, and a recurring or direct trigger over `case`, read live and unmasked at the run's start, `null` when the case is gone.
85. **Case steps (default).** Each through the case services in the effect transaction as `workflow-run`, reading every template (the system reader). `create_case` counts against `case-create` with the route's key (since Stage 4 it defers, item 95); `assign_case` with a policy is no change on an assigned case and when nobody is eligible; the `update_case_status` comment copy targets the run's newest comment; a run's `create_case` or field update refuses a `platform-file` field (no principal); `set_case_sla` refuses a case in a pausing status (`action_refused`) because its output names an expiry instant. `case_redacted` maps to `subject_redacted`; a code-less 422 to `action_input_invalid`. A field update repeats `updateCase`'s fields branch (`writeFieldsInTx`), because `api-cases` exports no `InTx` form.
86. **The review request (C128.7; default).** Users only (a key gets 403); the template is the one the inquiry's newest decline names in its contest route; 409 when none does; 410 for a redacted inquiry; `case-create` per environment; audit `inquiry.review-requested` with ids only; the statement goes through `addCommentInTx` as the relaying user's comment, so its mentions count. The publish refusal `contest_route_case_template_refused` is lifted (Stage 4 removes it from the error list, item 97).
87. **The graph's create-case (default).** Redacted and consolidated accounts are skipped and not held; nothing held, no case and `case-id: null`; the creator is the principal; `graph-query.action` audit metadata gains `case-id`.
88. **Holding a subject.** `CASE_HOLDS_SUBJECT` reads the stored event's case; the subject counts when the case holds it, or holds a report of that inquiry. References: `ctmpl`, `cqueue` and `cap` prefixes, members `case-template-id`, `case-queue-id`, `assignment-policy-id`; a policy has no status, so every policy counts as active. `seedCaseWorkflows` seeds a published "Review" template (key `REVIEW`).
89. **Existing tests changed with the plan:** `case-plumbing.test.ts` (db, API), `publish/rules.test.ts` (a case template in a contest route is taken), `case-routes.test.ts` (seven routes built), `services/cases/actions/service.test.ts`, `case-actions.test.ts` (reference kinds).

## Workflow 8B Stage 4 amendments (integration-8b)

Recorded by `integration-8b` on 2026-11-03, alone on the final tree. The evidence is `docs/tdd/phase8-integration-8b.tdd.md`.

### What Stage 4 settled

90. **A case is typed (C140.2, C140.5).** `OBJECT_VIEW_NAMES.case` is `'case'` (`packages/workflow-engine/src/variables/scope.ts`), so a case trigger, `current_case` and a fetched case read the case view: `status` and `resolution` plain, `fields` PII. "Review on report match" joins `CASE_RECIPE_ROWS`, so `GET /workflow-recipes`, the install route and `seed:dev` hold it; its draft passes the check with no error.
91. **The case-bulk job applies (C137.4).** The worker passed no publisher, so every bulk action stayed `pending`. `CaseBulkDeps` gets the worker's `events`, and `case-bulk-sweep` (60 s, `CASE_BULK_SWEEP_QUEUE`) calls `requeueUnfinishedCaseBulkActions` through the worker's own `case-bulk` producer (`WorkerCaseOptions.bulk`, `CaseRepeatableDeps.bulk`), for actions older than one period. The job id is the `cblk_` id, so a queued or running job is not doubled.
92. **Attachments reach the case's own routes (C129.2, C135.1, C139.1).** `caseRecord` (`services/cases/resources.ts`) reads `caseAttachmentRecordsOf`, filenames off for an event, so `GET /cases/:id`, lists, search, events and a run's view serve them; `updateCase` passes the PATCH store, stages `case.updated` once, and asks for the scan and rendition after the commit; `redactCasesInTx` calls `redactCaseAttachmentsInTx` on a first redaction and hands its keys back, which `RedactionService.redactCase` deletes after the commit.
93. **The API reads the scanner's name (C135.5, Stage 1b item 40).** Compose passes `OPENVERIFY_PROVIDER_ATTACHMENT_SCANNER_{SANDBOX,PRODUCTION}` to the API and the worker from one block; the clamd socket stays the worker's; `compose.test.sh` holds both to it (`ATTACHMENT_SCANNER` joins the kinds the API reads by name). The e2e stack binds `sandbox` for both.
94. **Case field keys have their own label (C136.4, P25).** `IDENTIFIER_KEY_LABELS['identifier-case-field']` (`openverify/identifier-case-field/v1`), with its OpenSSL vector; `caseFieldValueKey` uses it with the same input. `blind-index:rebuild` runs `rebuildCaseFieldKeys` (`services/cases/rebuild.ts`; `listCasesForRebuild`, `listCaseFieldKeys`) after the transaction field keys, under each case's row lock, leaving current keys alone. An install that wrote case keys before this item runs the rebuild once; nothing has been released, so no migration does it.
95. **`create_case` defers at `case-create` (C143.3, H30).** Its entry retries at 60, 300 and 900 seconds (provisional), so a step past the budget, or with a limiter that cannot answer, waits with nothing made instead of failing; the other case steps never retry. `steps.test.ts`' budget test changes with the plan.
96. **A defect: case events never reached a filtered webhook (C143.4).** `toEventSubject` (`events/subject.ts`) dropped `caseTemplateId` and `heldInquiryTemplateIds`, so a strict case subject failed every present `case-template-ids` filter and was judged by the any-one inquiry rule. Both pass on now. The C146.3 SLA spec found it: the event was written with no delivery.
97. **`contest_route_case_template_refused`** leaves `DEFINITION_CODES` and `docs/workflows/errors.md`, because no rule raises it.
98. **The OpenAPI snapshot** is regenerated once, for the description of `PUT /case-queues/:id/members` (cases go back at once, item 73); two writes are identical. `@openverify/node` needed no change.
99. **CI.** Each `unit-api` shard has `timeout-minutes: 40` (shard 1 took 22.3 of 30 on CI 41557289819, and 8B adds about 6% more API tests: estimated 23.6 to 25.2 minutes). The SDK host-test count is still 1,334, so the floor stands. No other job changes: `node` (14.3 of 20 on the baseline) gains about 100 non-API tests; `e2e` (18.6 of 70) gains four tests and about two minutes; `integration` (9.4 of 30) gains three files. Read the jobs on the run after this push.
100. **The full-stack suite** holds 117 tests in 61 files, shards of 39, 40 and 38 in 3.6, 4.9 and 4.0 minutes plus 1.1 for the stack, so N stays 3 and CI keeps one e2e job. The 8B specs: `case-review-recipe.spec.ts` (both paths, decision records not solely automated with `human-step: case-resolution`, the decline's basis filled), `case-sla.spec.ts` (`case.sla-expired` once to a template-filtered webhook, about 70 s), `case-attachments.spec.ts` (sandbox scan, a rendition without the GPS EXIF, the original keeping it).
101. **Docs.** `docs/cases/` (index, templates, queues, SLA, comments, attachments, export, redaction, workflows), the scanner in `docs/checks/sandbox-providers.md`, the renditions row in `services/vision/README.md`, the case specs in `e2e/README.md`.

### Carried forward

102. **Includes.** `GET /cases/:id?include=` answers 501 for `case-queue` and the seven held kinds; each needs its own scope and builder (C143.6).
103. **Emails.** No case email is sent: `notify-on-create` (P38), and the assigned, mentioned and SLA-breach emails (C133.7, C134.4, decision 76). The sweeps and services receive no mailer; the change is `CaseRepeatableDeps.mailer` from `worker.ts` and a link-only message beside each in-app notification.
104. **The status hook's publisher.** `CaseStatusChange` could carry the caller's publisher, so a pause past an unswept deadline dispatches at once instead of through the outbox sweeps (item 72).
105. **A drain job** for policies' `sort` and `sources` when an agent gains room (item 76); `compareUnassignedCases` is used by nothing yet.
106. **F30's measured budget.** A gzip budget of the built flow beside the module-graph guard (item 50).
107. **8A's `fetch_object` for its own types (HIGH, for 8C's `fix-api`).** The check types a fetched inquiry, account, report, transaction or verification as its flattened view, but 8A's rows answer the JSON:API resource, values under `attributes`; so `steps.x.output.object.status` publishes and reads nothing at run time, and a route on it silently takes `else`. The case row answers the flattened view (item 84); 8A's rows should too, with a test per type.
108. **8A's object actions on a case.** `getCustomFields` (`objects/fields.ts`) answers `{}` for a case, so `append`, `push` and `remove` start from empty; `tags.ts` and `update.ts` answer `applied` for a row lacking `tag` or `update` (`row.tag?.()`) and should fail closed.
109. **Step codes.** `platform_file_not_allowed` reads `unknown-error`; `case_redacted` is mapped to `subject_redacted`. `set_case_sla`'s output could make `sla-expires-at` nullable with `sla-state`, so a pausing status need not refuse.
110. **Named problems.** The transition-map and `search_*` codes are `errors[]` codes of a 422, not named problems; `attachment_scan_failed` (409) would name a scan error.
111. **Sweeps.** An export left `running` after a worker crash, and an attachment or file left `pending` when nobody asks again, recover only on a content request.
112. **Join tests (Stage 1b item 47).** The rendition hook at a replaced flow upload, `redact-biometrics` and a rejected handoff has no behaviour test.
113. **P22.** Image attachments on a case holding a selfie inquiry do not follow its biometric retention; a per-attachment variant of `redactCaseAttachmentRows` would serve it.
114. **P19's strict audit schemas** for the new case audit actions, with a coverage test.
115. **Performance.** `GET /cases` and search build each case with `getWorkflowCase` (about 17 queries a case); a page of 100 runs about 1,700 queries. Batch before release.
116. **`updateCaseFieldsInTx`.** The case row's field update repeats `updateCase`'s branch (item 85); `api-cases` could export one `InTx` form.
117. **A long PDF.** A multi-page guarded task would save a child start a page (item 56).
118. **The owner's review list:** every (default) above, first items 73 and 77 (members returned inline; the lock order), 66 (a run's copy empties a case's fields), 80 (scan error as a plain 409), 81 (export selection frozen as ids), 85 (`set_case_sla` refusing a pausing status), 86 (the review request's template).

## Orchestrator decisions after Workflow 8B (2026-11-03)

Binding on Workflow 8C. Each one comes from an 8B report or the 8C refresh.

1. **The console builders' gates (8C Stage 1).** A builder runs the dashboard's and the e2e package's lint and typecheck, the dashboard's tests, and its own full-stack spec files as one call that holds the e2e lock for the whole sequence: stack down, build, stack up, its specs, stack down. It runs no e2e gate, no shard and no other package's tests. In 8A, parallel agents ended each other's stacks and lost at least eight gate attempts, and 8B's sequence took 3 to 8 minutes, inside the 10-minute cap. The two fixers run every whole-repo gate on the merged tree, and the orchestrator's full validation follows. This moves gates and weakens none.
2. **Item 77's lock order is a defect, not only a default.** A deadlock between the queue paths and the case paths answers 500, so it is a known item for `fix-api` (K8-35): one lock order, or a bounded retry on `40P01` and `40001`, proven by a race test on Postgres.
3. **The privacy review reads `services/vision/src/openverify_vision/renditions.py`,** because stripping a file's metadata is a privacy property.
4. **The format gate's anchor is the owner's** (`docs/PROGRESS.md`, the 8B entry, owner item 6): a config-protection hook refuses edits of `.prettierignore` here, so no agent of 8C takes it.

## Workflow 8C Stage 1 amendments

Recorded by the 8C Stage 3 verifier from the contract deviations of the three Stage 1 reports. Evidence: `docs/tdd/phase8-dashboard-workflows.tdd.md`, `phase8-dashboard-cases.tdd.md` and `phase8-dashboard-reviewer.tdd.md`. Each item binds the 8C fixers and every later phase. Items marked (default) are on the owner's review list. `dashboard-workflows` ended `partial`: what it did not build (the editor, publishing, run actions, deliveries, test and manual runs, secrets, the C143.7 settings, the webhook filter fields, Trust Device and the other C141 settings, decision templates, problem and step-error words, the README section and `ui-workflows.spec.ts`) is not amended away by anything below; it stays C141's to build.

### dashboard-workflows

1. **Navigation (C141, C142).** Two groups follow Risk, so `/inquiries` stays the home page. "Cases" holds Cases (`/cases`, `cases:read`), Case templates (`/case-templates`, `case-templates:read`), Queues (`/case-queues`, `case-queues:read`) and Case settings (`/case-settings`, `case-queues:read`, the scope `GET /case-sla-policies` and `/case-calendars` ask). "Automation" holds Workflows (`/workflows`, `workflows:read`) and Secrets (`/secrets`, `secrets:read`). Each link shows only with its route's read scope. The console has 27 links in 7 groups; `nav-items.test.ts` and `nav.test.tsx` changed their counts and groups with the plan.
2. **The proxy matcher** leaves out only the case upload route, with or without a trailing slash: `'/((?!_next/static|_next/image|favicon.ico|cases/[^/]+/attachments/?$).*)'`. `/cases/:id/attachments/:catt` and every other case path stay behind the session guard.
3. **Mock routes have no registration point.** Each `testing/routes/<area>.ts` stands alone over `routeApi()`; `cases.ts` and `reviewer.ts` follow the same pattern.
4. **The inquiry page's decision records** are `InquiryDecisionsSection({ inquiryId, status, redacted, permissions })`, chained on the inquiry read inside the page's `Promise.all` because the reopen action needs the status. They load for every reader of the page, since `GET /decisions` asks the inquiry's own read scope, and render as the `SlotSection` "Decision records" before Details. Every test that renders the inquiry page serves a decision list; five existing tests gained one line each to serve an empty one, with no assertion changed.
5. **The account page's actions** are `AccountActionsSection({ accountId, permissions })`, loaded only with `workflows:trigger` and left out when the API lists none, rendered as the `SlotSection` "Workflow actions" before Details. `AccountView`'s prop is `workflowActions`, because `actions` already names the server actions.
6. **Console routes.** `/workflows`, `/workflows/[id]` and `/workflows/runs/[id]`; the static `runs` segment wins over `[id]`. Every run link is `/workflows/runs/<wfr_id>`. The audit log's `RESOURCE_PAGES` maps `workflow` to `/workflows` and `workflow-run` to `/workflows/runs`, and a `workflow-run` actor links its run page in the list and the entry.
7. **Decision record words (C128.1, C142.4).** `solely-automated` true reads "Yes: no person took part", false "No: a person took part on the path taken", and `null` "Not declared by the caller", never "no". A `null` comment reads "Redacted" for a redacted subject, "Hidden without pii:view" without `pii:view`, otherwise "None". An undeclared basis or contest route reads "Not declared". A contest route URL shows its host first (C128.7). An H-tier input shows its tier, stage and confidence; an R-tier input its tier.
8. **Run values (C141, H32).** A `null` run or step value reads "Redacted" when `redacted-at` is set, "Purged by retention" when `values-purged-at` is set, and "Hidden without pii:view" otherwise; an empty object reads "None". A run's status reads "Completed with N step errors (M outcomes unknown)", counting `failed-continued`, `timed-out` and `unknown-effect`; a step's unknown effect reads "Outcome unknown".
9. **Parameter forms** (account actions now; case actions, manual runs and test runs reuse `components/workflows/parameters.ts` and `parameter-fields.tsx`). A control is `parameter.<name>` with its declared type in a hidden `parameter-type.<name>`, typed by the C115.3 DSL: `number` and `integer` (an integer refuses a fraction), `boolean` as a three-way select (Not set, Yes, No) so an unset optional boolean is never sent as `false`, `datetime` converted to ISO 8601, `list<>` and `object{}` as JSON, the rest as text. An empty control is left out; a value that does not hold its type is refused at `/data/parameters/<name>` without calling the API. A masked prefill stays empty.
10. **Dependencies (C141.1).** `@xyflow/react` 12.12.0, `@dagrejs/dagre` 3.1.1 and `@panzoom/panzoom` 4.6.2 through the catalog, with no build script and no `minimumReleaseAgeExclude` entry. The d3 modules each had a release on the install day; the release-age policy resolved each to its 2021 or 2022 release, which is what the lockfile holds. `zustand` is 4.5.7. The `THIRD_PARTY_LICENSES.md` rows the report lists are `fix-api`'s to land (C144).
11. **Testing detail.** A test reads a Detail term by its `dt` text, because the term role takes no accessible name from content in the testing library.
12. **TDD gap recorded (rule 1).** The tests of `setWorkflowStatus` and `installRecipe` in `af40ae2c` were written after their GREEN, so no RED was seen for them. They stand; no later slice may cite them as RED evidence.
13. **Lists without a cursor (default).** `GET /workflow-recipes` is read whole (8A amendment 102). The workflow page reads its versions and runs 100 and 50 at a time, newest first, with no cursor; a `Pagination` with its own `param` and `label` (`versions-after`, `runs-after`) is owed before a workflow passes those counts.
14. **No API change.** No route, schema, `@openverify/node` method or API fixture changed for this agent's surfaces.

### dashboard-cases

15. **Routes (C142.1, default).** The list, bulk outcomes, notifications, exports and the download live under `/cases` (`/cases`, `/cases/bulk/<cblk_>`, `/cases/notifications`, `/cases/exports`, `/cases/exports/<cexp_>/download`); static segments win over the reviewer's `/cases/[id]`. Queues and assignment policies live under `/case-queues` (`/case-queues/<cqueue_>`, `/case-queues/policies`, `/case-queues/policies/<cap_>`); SLA policies and calendars under `/case-settings` (`/case-settings/sla-policies/<cslap_>`, `/case-settings/calendars/<ccal_>`), each list with its own cursor (`sla-after`, `calendars-after`). `app/cases/layout.tsx` gives every case page, the reviewer's included, the console layout, and no layout is added under `app/cases/[id]/`.
16. **Problems the shared mapper drops (C142.1, P21).** `displayProblemOf` (`lib/api/errors.ts`) keeps each error's pointer and detail but not its per-error `code` or any extension member. Until a shared change lets it keep them, `publishCaseTemplateDraft` reads the API's 422 itself and words each code at its place ("Status 4: ..."), keeping the API's detail for a code it does not know, and the calendar actions read `calendar_invalid`'s `dates` and name them.
17. **The case field subset in words (F37).** A file or JSON field is refused before the API is asked, through core's `caseFieldTypeProblems`, at `/data/attributes/field-schemas/<i>/type`, one message per field rather than one for the whole array.
18. **The version editor (C142.1, default).** Fields and statuses are JSON documents with errors at their pointers. The transition map is a grid of checkboxes over the saved statuses: a status added in the same save gets its moves after that save, and the help text says so. A box naming a status no longer sent is dropped, and every status is a key of the map, so an omitted one has no exits. A default module keeps the permissions it was saved with. A template's `visible-to-roles` and a version's `notify-on-create` are typed as ids.
19. **The case list (C142.1, default).** A row shows the status key with its category in words, because the list resource carries no status labels and reading each template version would cost a call per case. The assignee reads "You" or the operator id. Rows carry ids, keys and words, never a field value or a filename. The column choice is a browser-only preference under `openverify:case-list-columns`. Bulk set-status takes a typed status key, and a case whose template refuses the move is reported refused. Bulk outcomes are read on `/cases/bulk/<cblk_>`, which says while the job waits or runs that the outcomes are not known, with a refresh link and no polling.
20. **Next case (C132.3, ours).** On a list filtered by one queue the button reads "Next case from this queue" and sends `meta.case-queue-ids` with that queue; otherwise it pulls from all of the caller's queues.
21. **Exports (C138.1, default).** The form narrows by template, queue, status category and SLA state (the action also takes a status key); no search body is offered. Personal data needs `pii:view` and the form's `personal-data-confirmed=true`, both checked by the action before the API is called. The download is a GET route handler answering `text/csv` or `application/json` with `charset=utf-8`, `private, no-store`, and passing on 401, 403, 404 and 410 with no body.
22. **Calendars (C142.1, default).** Closures are shown read-only and kept as they are on save. Holidays are typed one per line as `YYYY-MM-DD label`. The two-year check's success is said in the save toast. A calendar has no delete route, so the e2e spec's calendar stays on the stack under a random name.
23. **The graph create-case button (C140.6).** Offered only with `cases:write` and `case-templates:read`, the scope it needs to list templates (`filter[status]=active`, keeping those with a published version); the queue choice is read with `case-queues:read`. `abilities.cases`, `caseTemplates` and `caseQueues` are optional, so Phase 7's `abilities` literals stand. The call goes through `api.graphQueries.action` with its Idempotency-Key, and the new case is linked by its console route, or the page says that no case was made.
24. **TDD gaps recorded (rule 1).** `components/cases/case-words.ts` was committed with the slice-1 stubs, without a RED of its own; its tables are exhaustive Records and later page tests exercise them. The RSC payload tests and `ui-cases.spec.ts` were written after their GREEN and passed at once; a mutation check showed the RSC test can fail. Two RED files of slice 2 changed for lint only inside the GREEN commit, which says so. No test was weakened.
25. **Axe is not run (C142.6).** The repository has no axe package. `@axe-core/playwright` brings `axe-core` (MPL-2.0), allowed only as unmodified test tooling under rule 4's exception with a `THIRD_PARTY_LICENSES.md` §5 row, added through the dependency owner. Until it lands, "axe on every page" is unmet for every 8C page; once it does, `fix-console` adds the check to `ui-cases.spec.ts` (`/cases`, a template version, a queue, `/case-settings`, `/cases/exports`) and to the other two agents' specs.

### dashboard-reviewer

26. **Inquiry images through renditions (C135.3).** `components/verification-image.tsx` renders every inquiry image view through the new `components/rendition-image.tsx` at `fileRenditionPath` (`/files/:id/rendition`), which says "Preparing the image…" politely, asks again after `Retry-After` up to 12 times, then offers "Try again". The PDF download keeps `/files/:id/content`. Six existing tests outside the agent's paths changed only their asserted image path, because C135.3 changes the behaviour they check: `selfie-evidence`, `verification-card`, `evidence/document`, `evidence/government-id-nfc`, `app/inquiries/[id]/page` and `app/verifications/[id]/page` (named in `51e7dcea`).
